#2425. San Diego vs Sporting Kansas City - prediction, team news, lineups

By Joel Lefevre | 04 Nov 2026 23:22, Last updated: 05 Nov 2026 20:43

Needing a win to have any hope of avoiding a dead-last finish in MLS this year, Sporting Kansas City wrap up the regular season on Saturday with a visit to Snapdragon Stadium to face San Diego.

Both teams have been eliminated from post-season contention, with San Diego sitting 13th in the West after a 1-0 win over St. Louis City and KC dead last despite a 1-1 draw at home to Dallas.

San Diego were 90 minutes away from the MLS Cup final last year but have not been able to replicate that success in year two.

Mikey Varas’s side can finish 12th at best in the West this season, but they must win and hope that Minnesota United lose on Decision Day to surpass them.

They can eclipse 40 points with a triumph on Saturday, though San Diego would still boast far fewer points than they had in their inaugural MLS campaign in 2025 (63).

With a win this weekend, they would match their 2025 regular-season home total, when they claimed seven victories on home soil.

While they have scored in all but one of their regular-season home fixtures this year, this team have also conceded multiple goals in 10 MLS outings this season at Snapdragon Stadium.

In their previous three meetings versus SKC, San Diego have yet to lose, while failing to concede a single goal against them.

Mercifully, the 2026 campaign will come to an end for Sporting Kansas City on Saturday in San Diego.

The former two-time MLS Cup champions are already assured of finishing this campaign with fewer points than ever before, as a win on Saturday would put them at 26 overall.

A victory combined with a CF Montreal defeat and a big goal swing in SKC's favour, is the only thing that will enable them to avoid finishing dead last in the league for the first time.

Raphael Wicky’s men have already set a single-season league record for goals allowed in this competition (81), conceding in all but one MLS affair this year.

It may hardly be a consolation, but with a victory on Decision Day, Kansas City would surpass their number of away wins in this competition from a season ago (three).

Sporting have lost two of their three regular season meetings this year in California, conceding a combined eight goals over that stretch.

Sporting Kansas City Major League Soccer form:

A lower body injury will likely keep Elias Achouri out of the San Diego fold in this meaningless game on Saturday.

Anders Dreyer scored in the latter stages of the first half last week, with Pablo Sisniega stopping seven shots to collect a clean sheet.

At SKC, Lasse Berg Johnsen, Seymour Reid, Moises Mosquera and Zorhan Bassong are all questionable due to hamstring strains, Justin Reynolds is dealing with a sore quad, Diego Borges has an adductor issue and Kwaku Agyabeng is doubtful because of an ankle injury.

Jansen Miller scored the equaliser for them on matchday 33, the first in this competition for the eighth overall pick in the 2025 MLS SuperDraft.

Sisniega; Thorhallsson, McVey, Murphy, Sargent; Tverskov, Pirani, Valakari; Dreyer, Ingvartsen, Zamble

Sporting Kansas City possible starting lineup:

Cleveland; Davis, Miller, Meyer, Karic; Luiz, Bartlett, Wolff, Harris; Calheira, Afrifa

Kansas City allow far too many quality chances to their opponents, and we doubt they can shut down a side with San Diego’s firepower, even in this meaningless game.

#2421. Liverpool transfer news: Andoni Iraola could lose £15m attacker to Premier League 'admirers' after disastrous spell

By Lewis Nolan | 04 Nov 2026 23:24

Liverpool attacker Harvey Elliot could sign for Leeds United permanently in the January transfer window, the latest report has claimed.

The Reds attacker is currently on loan at Valencia, though he has not enjoyed a particularly productive spell in Spain, with the 23-year-old yet to start for the club this season.

Elliott was in fact sent off against Villarreal on October 24, and rumours have suggested that Valencia could end his loan this winter.

Liverpool boss Andoni Iraola did not show that he trusted the Englishman during pre-season, and with his contract set to expire in summer 2028, a permanent exit may make sense.

Football Insider claim that Leeds United would be prepared to move for him in the January transfer window if an opportunity arose.

Elliott is valued by Transfermarkt at just £15m, but it would be surprising if the Reds managed to recoup anywhere close to that fee in the winter.

However, the attacker is unlikely to earn any minutes for Iraola, whose system requires a level of intensity that he has not yet shown he is capable of, and sitting on the bench would only further deteriorate his value.

While a new loan should not be ruled out, it remains to be seen if Elliott would be open to another temporary exit given his move to Aston Villa last season was also disastrous.

If Leeds show any interest in the 23-year-old this January, the Reds should facilitate a sale, even if the fee they receive is relatively modest.

One of the reasons for Elliott's struggles is that he has been largely overlooked since 2024, with managers like Unai Emery and Arne Slot not even using him as a rotation option.

The 23-year-old played just 371 minutes of Premier League football in 2024-25, and he played 113 minutes of league football last season.

While Elliott must bear responsibility for some of the fitness issues that have been raised during his time in Spain, it is understandable that he is not at his best considering his turbulent last two years.

#2420. Valentin Carboni, dopo l'accordo Inter-Racing arriva la decisione inattesa

Valentin Carboni, dopo l'accordo Inter-Racing arriva la decisione inattesa

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Sembrava ormai a un passo dal ritorno in campo, ma Valentin Carboni dovrà aspettare ancora. Il talento argentino, di proprietà dell'Inter e attualmente in prestito al Racing Avellaneda, non ha ancora ottenuto il via libera dei medici dopo il grave infortunio al ginocchio subito lo scorso febbraio.

Nelle ultime settimane il classe 2005 aveva intensificato gli allenamenti, tornando anche a svolgere alcune esercitazioni insieme ai compagni. Segnali incoraggianti che avevano alimentato la speranza di rivederlo tra i convocati già per la partita contro il Barracas Central.

Alla fine, però, è arrivata una decisione diversa.

Secondo quanto riferisce Racing de Alma, il corpo medico e lo staff tecnico guidato da Chirola Romero hanno deciso di non accelerare i tempi. Carboni non è ancora considerato pronto per disputare una partita ufficiale e resterà quindi fuori dalla lista dei convocati.

Il giocatore sta completando la fase conclusiva della riabilitazione dopo la rottura del legamento crociato anteriore del ginocchio destro, rimediata durante un allenamento lo scorso febbraio.

La sua evoluzione viene giudicata positivamente, ma i medici non intendono concedere il via libera prima di avere garanzie sufficienti sulle condizioni fisiche del calciatore.

L'obiettivo è evitare una possibile ricaduta dopo un infortunio particolarmente delicato.

Non si tratta dunque di un nuovo problema fisico, ma di una scelta prudenziale che potrebbe allungare ulteriormente il periodo di inattività del giovane argentino.

I nerazzurri hanno già raggiunto un accordo con il Racing per prolungare il prestito di Carboni fino al 31 dicembre 2027, consentendo al giocatore di restare in Argentina per un'altra stagione.

Una decisione maturata proprio alla luce del lungo stop che ha compromesso il suo 2026.

Carboni, arrivato al Racing a gennaio, ha infatti disputato appena cinque partite ufficiali prima di essere fermato dall'infortunio.

Adesso la priorità è recuperare completamente e ritrovare la migliore condizione atletica. La possibilità di rivederlo in campo entro la fine dell'anno si è progressivamente ridotta e, secondo le ultime indiscrezioni argentine, il rientro potrebbe essere rimandato alla preparazione per la stagione 2027.

Nessuna data definitiva è stata ancora fissata, ma al Racing prevale una linea molto chiara: meglio aspettare qualche settimana in più che rischiare di compromettere nuovamente il recupero del giocatore.

#2412. Man United transfer news: Scouts 'intensify' efforts to sign £38m defender as Michael Carrick endures crisis in the backline

By Lewis Nolan | 05 Nov 2026 00:27

Manchester United are in the race for Como defender Jacobo Ramon Naveros, the newest report has revealed.

It is understandable that the Red Devils are already looking forward to the next few transfer windows considering the club are 17th in the Premier League table with just seven points.

Michael Carrick's side are only above the relegation zone because of goal difference, and part of the reason they are so low is because of their defence.

United have conceded at least two goals five times in the Premier League so far this season, and the squad are in desperate need of reinforcements at the back.

A new report from TEAMtalk claims that United's scouting of Como defender Ramon is set to intensify, especially following his game against the Red Devils in the Champions League in October.

Ramon has primarily operated as a centre-back during his career, and the defender has shown that he can contribute at both ends of the pitch.

The 21-year-old managed to score against United after he rotated into midfield and decided to make an unchallenged run into the penalty area, before heading home from close range.

There are no question marks about the Spaniard's ability on the ball, but he also routinely wins his individual battles at the other end of the pitch, with the centre-back coming out on top in 67% of his duels in Serie A this season.

Though Ramon is valued by Transfermarkt at €45m (£37.51m), United will almost certainly have to pay significantly more than that price, but his potential is high.

It may sound hyperbolic, but United have one of the Premier League's weakest defences, with the likes of Luke Shaw and Harry Maguire far beyond their best.

Lisandro Martinez's mentality has been questioned, while many fans are keen for right-back Diogo Dalot to be replaced.

The need to strengthen at the back is urgent, and given United arguably find themselves in a relegation battle, they cannot afford to ignore their defence any longer.

#2408. Fenerbahce 3-2 Liverpool: Trey Nyoni goal sets record but Champions League defeat reveals January transfer window reality

By Lewis Nolan | 05 Nov 2026 01:26

Liverpool midfielder Trey Nyoni became the youngest player in the club's history to score his first goal for the team in a European Cup match (19 years, 127 days).

Though the midfielder excelled once again, his side ultimately lost 3-2 against Fenerbahce on Wednesday, with the Reds suffering their first Champions League loss of the campaign.

Andoni Iraola was frustrated by how his side conceded all three goals, arguing that they should have done more to prevent their opponents scoring from set pieces.

If there was a positive for the Reds, it would be that Nyoni managed to net his first goal for the club, opening the scoring in the 25th minute.

Opta revealed that the teenager became the youngest player to score their first goal for Liverpool in the Champions League or European Cup.

19 - Trey Nyoni is the youngest player in Liverpool's history to score his first goal for the club in a European Cup match (19 years, 127 days). Kicks. https://t.co/9Hb4p0SnBx

Nyoni may have ended up on the losing side on Wednesday, but he was arguably the best player on the pitch, and he has made a strong claim for a starting sport moving forward.

However, relying on the 19-year-old would be unfair, and if Iraola wants to claim silverware this season and in the coming campaigns, he will almost certainly need to delve into the transfer market.

Alexis Mac Allister has played well in the vast majority of games this term, but there are still concerns about his long-term future.

While Dominik Szoboszlai has often been used in a double pivot under Iraola, his displays have been alarming, as have Ryan Gravenberch's.

If Liverpool want to avoid overplaying Nyoni while maintaining a high standard, they must look to bring in a new midfielder in the winter transfer window.

Monaco star Lamine Camara has been strongly linked with a move to Anfield, and after his failed switch to Chelsea, there could be an opportunity to sign him in the winter.

The 22-year-old is strong with and without the ball, though there is an argument that the Merseysiders may need another midfielder if Mac Allister left in the summer.

Bournemouth's Alex Scott has played under Iraola, but given he has been linked with Chelsea, Liverpool should be ready to move onto a different target.

Allo stesso tempo, non vedo altre squadre in grado di impensierirla più di tanto. La Lazio difficilmente riuscirà a mantenere questo ritmo. Al di là del fatto che stia facendo molto bene e giochi un ottimo calcio, credo sia quasi impossibile continuare così fino alla fine. È vero che non ha le coppe, ma non la considero una squadra strutturata per sostenere un simile rendimento per tutto il campionato. Mi auguro che nelle ultime due o tre giornate ci siano almeno un paio di squadre a contendersi il titolo, altrimenti il finale rischia di diventare monotono. Un po' di adrenalina sarebbe l'ideale".

L'Inter non vince un derby da due anni e mezzo: come spieghi questa difficoltà?

"È una situazione piuttosto strana. Non credo che il Milan sia una vera e propria bestia nera dell'Inter, né che ci sia una particolare situazione tattica o psicologica. Il derby è certamente una partita particolare, ma i giocatori nerazzurri sono abituati a gestire appuntamenti importanti, come quelli in Champions League. Non credo sia nemmeno un merito esclusivo del Milan, perché anche i rossoneri cambiano ogni anno. Secondo me è una coincidenza legata alla particolarità del derby, una serie di episodi sfavorevoli. Semplicemente, può capitare una giornata storta. E all'Inter, ultimamente, capita proprio contro il Milan".

Dopo le ultime vittorie in Champions, dove può arrivare questa Inter in Europa? Può competere con le grandi?

"L'Inter ha vinto due partite consecutive, ma non è diventata improvvisamente una squadra straordinaria, così come non era diventata scarsa quando aveva perso. Bisogna mantenere equilibrio nei giudizi. Può giocarsela con le grandi squadre? Certamente, lo ha dimostrato anche negli anni passati. Lasciando da parte le finali perse, il fatto stesso di arrivarci dimostra il valore della squadra. In Champions League ogni partita è una storia a sé, soprattutto in una competizione che prevede sfide secche. Bisogna valutare il momento in cui si affronta una determinata squadra, le condizioni dei giocatori e gli eventuali infortuni. Quando si arriverà alle partite di andata e ritorno, il discorso cambierà ulteriormente. In una gara secca, invece, può capitare di affrontare una squadra sulla carta più forte, ma in un momento di difficoltà o con qualche assenza importante. Allo stesso modo, una squadra inferiore può incontrare l'Inter in condizioni favorevoli".

#2402. I tifosi del Genoa caricano l'ambiente: "Il Milan avrà clima ostile, non ci siamo dimenticati certi episodi"

Il pubblico del grifone sembra esser piuttosto caldo in vista della sfida contro il Milan, in programma domenica alle 15.00 al Ferraris di Genova. In un lungo messaggio social, il tifo organizzato rossoblù di espone duramente contro i rossoneri, invitando tutto lo stadio a creare un clima ostile ai ragazzi di mister Amorim:

"Domenica cantiamo con rabbia, non dimentichiamoci chi abbiamo davanti. A volte ci si dimentica il significato di clima ostile. Premessa importante, sono un tifoso come un altro, non ho intenzione di imporre nulla a nessuno, e ci mancherebbe altro. In ogni caso ero curioso di sapere il pensiero dei Genoani sul "discorso bandiere". Nel match contro la Juventus, tralasciando la grande prestazione dei ragazzi in campo, c'è stato uno stadio, e soprattutto una Nord, diversa dal solito, anche grazie al leggero minor sventolamento delle bandiere durante la partita. Sicuramente in parte la bolgia è stata alimentata dalla partita di spessore, e dal risultato, tuttavia in molte occasioni, con la gente coinvolta ai fatti di campo, si è venuto a creare un clima ostile per gli avversari e di carica per i nostri. Tanto per dirne una, quando Osmajic ha messo giù quella palla per poi tirare in porta sull'esterno della rete, si è creata una bolgia dal nulla, tutto perché la Nord è riuscita a vedere che cosa stava succedendo. È questo che fa la differenza per un giocatore in campo, e non il cantare come uno stereo di sottofondo senza nemmeno capire se stiamo attaccando, se abbiamo conquistato un calcio d'angolo, o se l'arbitro ci sta dando contro. Poi ci sono persone che in Nord non cantano in entrambi i casi e questo è ur discorso a parte, tuttavia con un muro di bandiere costante (letteralmente nella Nord bassa non si vede quasi nulla) anche la parte di persone che, invece, è intenzionata a creare il clima ostile e cantare, o fischiare, si ritrova alienata da tutto e magari canta si, ma in modo da non fare la differenza emotiva per un giocatore in campo che, per esempio, sta recuperando su un avversario. E bello sventolare le bandiere, è un bel colpo d'occhio, però sarebbe bello farlo nei momenti più opportuni, come ai tempi della Fossa, ovviamente valutando partita per partita. Siamo lì anche e soprattutto per aiutare il Genoa a vincere".

#2401. Allegri a Sport TV: “La squadra si è disunita dopo il 2-1. Assenze? Non ci hanno permesso di cambiare l’inerzia”

Massimiliano Allegri, tecnico del Napoli, ha rilasciato alcune dichiarazioni a Sport TV a margine della sconfitta in Champions subita per 4-1 contro il Porto di Francesco Farioli. Ecco le sue parole: “Abbiamo affrontato una buona squadra, l’FC Porto. Il Napoli ha giocato bene per 70 minuti; poi, sul 2-1, la squadra si è disunita. #2399. Predicting Thomas Tuchel's England squad for Croatia, Spain UEFA Nations League fixtures

By Ben Sully | 05 Nov 2026 02:38, Last updated: 05 Nov 2026 03:32

England will complete the 2026-27 Nations League league phase with fixtures against Croatia and Spain this month.

The Three Lions will welcome Croatia to Wembley on November 12 before they head to Atletico Madrid's Metropolitano for a test against the world champions on November 15.

Thomas Tuchel's side head into the final two fixtures in second place in Group A3 after taking nine points from their first four matches since finishing in third position at the 2026 World Cup.

After starting with a narrow 3-2 defeat against Spain at Wembley, England recorded three consecutive victories by a combined 12-0 scoreline to all but seal a quarter-final berth.

The Three Lions will look to mathematically secure a last-eight spot and challenge Spain for the honour of clinching first place.

Ahead of Tuchel's squad announcement on Friday, November 6, Sports Mole predicts which players will make the cut for the final two matches of the Nations League league phase.

There appears to be little uncertainty surrounding the first two names on what is expected to be a three-man list of goalkeepers.

Everton’s Jordan Pickford and Leeds United’s James Trafford are guaranteed to receive the call-up from Tuchel, with the real focus on whether Tuchel will pick a number one for this international window or share the game time like he did in the opening four Nations League matches.

Pickford has long been seen as England’s number one, but he had to watch the opening two games against Spain and the Czech Republic from the bench as Tuchel offered Trafford the chance to make his case to be his first-choice goalkeeper.

Trafford then made way for Pickford to feature in the 7-0 away win over Croatia and the 3-0 home victory against the Czech Republic.

While his position may not be as certain as it once was, Pickford continues to close in on a century of England caps, with the 32-year-old just seven appearances away from reaching that impressive milestone.

In regard to the third-choice goalkeeper, Dean Henderson is set to get the nod over Brighton & Hove Albion’s Jason Steele after recently making his return to action from an ankle injury.

Predicted England goalkeepers for upcoming Nations League fixtures: Dean Henderson, Jordan Pickford, James Trafford

Tuchel initially picked nine defenders for the last international window before Newcastle United’s Tino Livramento pulled out due to injury.

Livramento remains unavailable for selection, along with England’s first-choice right-back Reece James, who is expected to be out until the new year after undergoing knee surgery.

In more positive news, Djed Spence, who was one of the standout players of England’s World Cup campaign, is back in contention after making six appearances for Inter Milan since returning from injury.

Trent Alexander-Arnold, meanwhile, has struggled for game time in recent weeks due to competition from Federico Valverde and Denzel Dumfries.

However, he could still get the nod for England after impressing in his two appearances in the last international window.

Jarell Quansah can also cover at right-back as well as offering an option in central defence, while Lewis Hall and Nico O’Reilly are likely to be the two left-backs in the squad.

Ezri Konsa, Marc Guehi and Jarrad Branthwaite are widely expected to be among Tuchel’s central defensive options, while injuries to John Stones and Trevoh Chalobah could open the door for Levi Colwill, who has started eight of Chelsea’s nine Premier League games this season.

Dan Burn is another name who could be in the mix, but while he played a useful role at the World Cup, Tuchel may be reluctant to bring the 34-year-old back into the fold as he keeps one eye on his plans for the European Championship in 2028.

Predicted England defenders for upcoming Nations League fixtures: Trent Alexander-Arnold, Jarrad Branthwaite, Levi Colwill, Marc Guehi, Lewis Hall, Ezri Konsa, Nico O’Reilly, Jarell Quansah, Djed Spence

The main headline news for England's midfield options is the imminent return of Declan Rice, who missed the last international window due to load management.

Rice is expected to form a central midfield pairing with Elliot Anderson, a player who continues to grow week by week under Enzo Maresca at Manchester City.

Myles Lewis-Skelly started three of the first four Nations League fixtures in Rice's absence, suggesting that Tuchel sees him more as a central midfielder than a left-back.

The Arsenal man should make the cut again, although he may have to settle for a substitute role this time around.

Alex Scott is someone who is almost definitely set to miss out on selection despite impressing in his 70-minute display against Croatia last month.

The Bournemouth star picked up a thigh injury in the emphatic win and has since missed seven games for the Cherries.

While he is close to a return to action, the November international window will come too soon, which may open the door for Jordan Henderson to make the squad for the first time since suffering a wrist injury at the World Cup.

James Garner was called up as a replacement for Kobbie Mainoo in September, and he could retain his spot ahead of the Manchester United midfielder, who has only recently returned from injury.

Looking at attacking midfielders, Jude Bellingham will be one of the first names on Tuchel's list, and he is set to join the national team in a positive mood after scoring in Real Madrid's last two matches.

Morgan Gibbs-White was a late call-up for the last squad, but is likely to miss out this time due to his underwhelming form in a struggling Nottingham Forest side.

There is a possibility that Tuchel may look to the future with Fulham star Josh King, who is an outside contender for a call-up after firmly establishing himself as a key player at Fulham this term.

Predicted England midfielders for upcoming Nations League fixtures: Elliot Anderson, Jude Bellingham, James Garner, Jordan Henderson, Myles Lewis-Skelly, Declan Rice

The front three of Bukayo Saka, Anthony Gordon and Harry Kane scored 11 goals between them across the first four UEFA Nations League fixtures as the trio linked up to clinical effect.

Unfortunately, it is a forward line that is guaranteed to be broken following the news that Saka is set for a spell on the sidelines with a thigh injury.

Noni Madueke has stepped up for Arsenal in Saka’s absence and is likely to be asked to do the same for England’s final two league phase matches.

Madueke’s Arsenal teammate Eberechi Eze is someone who appears to be trusted by Tuchel and should be on the list when the squad announcement is made on Friday, especially as another left-sided option, Marcus Rashford, is unavailable due to injury.

Liverpool’s exciting youngster Rio Ngumoha is also set to make the cut after making his first two competitive international appearances in the away and home games against the Czech Republic.

Meanwhile, Chelsea’s Morgan Rogers will be full of confidence when he joins the England squad after netting three goals in his previous five appearances.

Rogers appears to be stealing the limelight from his friend and teammate Cole Palmer, who appeared to frustrate Tuchel when he pulled out of his most recent squad with a minor issue.

The England boss may be tempted to offer Palmer another chance, especially with Saka sidelined, but it is clear that he has doubts over the strength of the 24-year-old’s desire to play for his country.

In regard to the centre-forward spot, England captain Harry Kane is a guaranteed starter and, barring injury, he will break his country’s all-time appearance record against Croatia at Wembley.

The biggest question is who will provide cover for the 2026 Ballon d’Or winner, with England struggling to find someone who can get anywhere near the level Kane produces.

Calvert-Lewin is not operating at the same elite level as Kane, but he has scored four goals in nine Premier League appearances this season and is likely to be the backup striker for the upcoming international window.

Tuchel is expected to only pick two strikers, but if he wants to add a third to his squad, he could look to the Championship where Middlesbrough’s Will Lankshear is the leading scorer with 13 goals to his name.

The in-form striker has been a regular for the England Under-21s, but with qualifying for next summer’s Under-21 European Championship all wrapped up, it could be a good opportunity to offer him a taste of life in the senior setup.

Predicted England attackers for upcoming Nations League fixtures: Dominic Calvert-Lewin, Eberechi Eze, Anthony Gordon, Harry Kane, Noni Madueke, Rio Ngumoha, Cole Palmer, Morgan Rogers

#2398. Rambaudi avvisa la Lazio: “Non devi andare a Napoli e pensare di gestire la partita”

Le analisi e i consigli di Roberto Rambaudi in vista del big match di Serie A fra Lazio e Napoli

In vista della sfida contro il Napoli, l'ex giocatore della Lazio Roberto Rambaudi ha analizzato a Radiosei la pesante assenza di Mattia Zaccagni e l'approccio tattico e mentale con cui la squadra capitolina dovrà affrontare il match al Maradona.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Zaccagni è quello che salta l'uomo, fa assist, crea superiorità; sarà un test per chi prende il suo posto. Ad ogni modo, nonostante il Napoli sia in difficoltà, la vittoria non è mai scontata. Sono forti ma non sono nella condizione mentale ottimale. Immagino la squadra verrà contestata; alcuni giocatori, i più importanti e rappresentativi, proveranno a dare una scossa, in casa, prima della sosta. Non devi andare a Napoli e pensare di gestire la partita, credo che molto si deciderà nei primi 20-25', se li fai galvanizzare o se li metti subito in difficoltà, creando dall'inizio.

Se Tavares e Pedraza stanno bene, potrebbe essere un'opzione vedere loro due dal 1' a sinistra. Credo sia una scelta che Gattuso farà in base a come vede i calciatori durante la settimana. Magari anche Cancellieri a sinistra può essere una soluzione, io Isaksen da destra non lo toglierei.

#2396. Verso Genoa-Milan: tutti i numeri e curiosità della partita

Dopo la pessima figuraccia fatta in Europa League contri gli ungheresi del Ferencvàros, il Milan di Amorim dovrà assolutamte rialzare la testa e proseguire nel proprio positivo percorso in campionato. La sfida in programma domenica alle 15.00 non è delle più semplici: il diavolo sarà ospite del Genoa di Daniele De Rossi, oggi in un ottimo periodo di forma visti i recenti risultati in Serie A.

Il Milan è imbattuto nelle ultime 10 sfide di Serie A contro il Genoa (6V, 4N); dal 2020/21, tra le squadre contro cui non hanno mai perso nel massimo campionato, i rossoneri hanno disputato più incontri solamente contro l'Hellas Verona (12), inoltre il Milan ha vinto tutte le ultime quattro trasferte di Serie A contro il Genoa; solo contro tre squadre i rossoneri vantano attualmente una striscia aperta di successi esterni più lunga nella competizione: Hellas Verona (sette), Messina (cinque) e Pisa (cinque).

Mentre il Genoa è reduce da tre vittorie consecutive in Serie A; l'ultima volta che i rossoblù hanno registrato una striscia di quattro successi di fila in una singola stagione nel massimo campionato risale al periodo tra marzo e aprile 2009. Inoltre, il grifone di De Rossi  ha vinto tre delle prime sei partite casalinghe in questo campionato (1N, 2P); i rossoblù non ottengono quattro successi nelle prime sette gare interne di Serie A dalla stagione 2015/16, quando in panchina sedeva Gian Piero Gasperini.

#2395. Willem II vs PEC Zwolle - prediction, team news, lineups

By Adepoju Marvellous | 05 Nov 2026 04:34

Willem II and PEC Zwolle kick off round 12 of the 2026-27 Eredivisie season with a clash at the Koning Willem II Stadion on Friday night.

The hosts are still searching for their first league win of the campaign, while the visitors aim to extend their unbeaten league run to four matches.

Willem II begin life under Jurgen Streppel after the 57-year-old’s recent appointment as head coach, and they will hope their new manager can hit the ground running, especially given their sluggish start to the season.

De Superkruiken claimed their first win of 2026-27 with a 4-3 triumph over Helmond Sport in the KNVB Beker, but then suffered a humiliating 7-0 defeat to reigning champions PSV Eindhoven—taking their loss tally to nine in 11 Eredivisie outings.

Already 11 points adrift of safety, Willem II are paying the price for one of the longest winless starts to an Eredivisie season. As the campaign enters its second third, they require a dramatic turnaround in fortunes to avoid the drop.

Three points against Zwolle would be the perfect start to Streppel’s reign, but Friday’s hosts have struggled at home against their upcoming opponents, winning just one of the last five meetings (L3, D1).

With 26 of their 39 goals conceded coming after half-time, Willem II’s fate could well be determined by their second-half performance. The prospect of facing a team that has scored just a third of its 12 goals in the opening 45 minutes offers little encouragement.

Though not as poor as Willem II, Zwolle also made a poor start to their league campaign, securing just one win from their opening eight matches. However, they have since won two of their last three, either side of a 1-1 draw with Utrecht.

Sandwiched between those three unbeaten league matches was a 2-1 cup defeat to lower-league opposition - arguably the most disappointing result of the season for De Blauwvingers. Nonetheless, they travel to Tilburg with renewed confidence.

Zwolle remain in search of a new head coach following Henry van den Vegt’s departure. In the meantime, caretaker manager Dennis van der Ree is gradually making a case for himself, as his tenure has coincided with an upturn in results.

A victory over Willem II in their final match before the international break would undoubtedly strengthen calls from the Zwolle fanbase for Van der Ree to be appointed permanently. The 47-year-old will be eager to bolster his reputation after a difficult spell with Groningen.

No team in the Eredivisie has converted a lower percentage of their big chances than Friday’s visitors (20.8%), highlighting a key area for improvement if they are to sustain their upturn in form.

Jaden Slory served a one-match suspension during Willem II’s defeat to PSV last time out, but the 21-year-old is available for selection here.

This leaves Justin Hoogma and Amine Lackhar, currently sidelined with knee and groin injuries, respectively, as the hosts’ two guaranteed absences.

Eighteen-year-old Givaro Rahajaan has not featured in a matchday squad since early September and remains out of contention for PEC Zwolle.

Sherel Floranus will miss a fourth consecutive match for the visitors, who are also without Filip Krastev due to an Achilles injury.

Younes Namli found the net in the most recent meeting between these sides, and the Dane will be aiming to make an impact once again.

Delanghe; Ter Avest, Stam, Dekker, Tjoe a On; Twigt, Zarrouk; Slory, Van Aalst, Van Crooy; Haen

Schendelaar; Van de Haar, Viergever, Graves, Aertssen; Sommer, Thomas, Van der Wal; Mbayo, Van Hooijdonk, Namli

Back in the Eredivisie after 11 years, new Willem II boss Streppel is tasked with pulling off what would be one of the biggest escapes in league history.

Given Zwolle’s current form and Willem II’s ongoing defensive frailties, the visitors are narrowly tipped to come out on top.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Ha un sapore veramente amaro, e non soltanto dal punto di vista della prestazione. Il Napoli ancora una volta si sgretola, crolla e va incontro a una battuta d’arresto che lascia scorie pesanti tra i protagonisti. È questa la vera notizia della serata Saudita Poi Veiga è tornato in Europa e sta facendo benissimo con la maglia del Porto. C’è poi il capitolo post-partita. Poco prima, Matteo Politano ci aveva messo la faccia, dicendo senza filtri: “Dobbiamo solo scusarci e lavorare più duro. Sono arrabbiatissimo”. Poi, parlando ai microfoni di Sky, ha aggiunto: “Ogni tanto ci mettiamo la faccia sempre noi italiani, vorrei sentire anche qualche altro compagno”. Un messaggio forte che evidentemente è stato ricevuto. Poco dopo, infatti, arriva Scott McTominay: “Sono arrabbiato, non c’è niente da sorridere, sono veramente troppo arrabbiato per riuscire a descriverlo”. la questione dei contratti, un tema molto delicato in questo momento. Il Napoli sta lavorando da tempo al rinnovo di Scott McTominay, con grande fiducia tra settembre e ottobre. un passaggio fondamentale, perché il presente scotta, ma la programmazione passa da decisioni di questo calibro. Dall’altro lato c’è chi vede già la porta d’uscita. Prendiamo il caso di Frank Zambo Anguissa, che ha vissuto un inizio di stagione altalenante. La società quest’estate ha deciso di trattenerlo, ma con il contratto in scadenza, l’addio a parametro zero nell’estate del 2027 rappresenta oggi un’ipotesi molto concreta da monitorare attentamente”.

#2387. Cagliari vs. Frosinone - prediction, team news, lineups

By Seye Omidiora | 05 Nov 2026 05:57

Winless at Cagliari since 2013, Frosinone head into Saturday's gameweek-11 fixture at Unipol Domus as the likely favourites.

The promoted side have thrived since returning to the top flight, sitting sixth in the Serie A table ahead of another visit to a ground where they have not won in over a decade.

Cagliari's start to the 2026-27 campaign has been mixed, with the Sardinians experiencing contrasting fortunes across the opening rounds.

Fabio Pisacane's men claimed four victories from their opening five matches, suffering an understandable home defeat to Inter Milan, before drawing 1-1 with Juventus in gameweek six.

That point appeared encouraging in isolation, but the Isolani have failed to collect any more since, suffering four consecutive defeats to Monza, Bologna, Roma and Lazio while notably surrendering leads against both capital clubs.

Those results leave Pisacane's men 12th in the Serie A table, although they could climb as high as eighth if the teams above them, Napoli, Bologna, Torino and Genoa, all drop points.

However, that outcome depends on the Sardinians ending their league losing streak against an in-form, high-flying visiting side.

Frosinone's return to the top flight after two years brought the customary uncertainty, but the Canaries have flourished in the big time.

They offered signs of what was to come during their opening-day defeat to Juventus at Stadio Benito Stirpe, where the Old Lady claimed a 1-0 victory.

Only Napoli have beaten Massimiliano Alvini's side since, with the Canarini securing six victories from their next nine matches to sit sixth in the Serie A table ahead of matchday 11.

Their only away league defeat remains last month's 3-0 hammering at the Maradona in Naples, while the Ciociari have claimed a 3-0 victory over Fiorentina and earned satisfying draws at Genoa and Atalanta, the latter finishing 4-4 in Bergamo.

If there were grounds for criticism of Alvini's troops, it would be their failure to capitalise on Genoa and Atalanta's underwhelming form when facing those clubs, with Daniele De Rossi's men sitting 15th in the table and the Bergamo outfit two places below.

Nonetheless, they enter Saturday's encounter seeking their first away top-flight victory since August, a result that would also end their 13-year wait for success at Cagliari.

Adam Obert will miss this weekend's clash after receiving a late red card for two bookable offences against Lazio last time out.

Alessandro Deiola and Roberto Gagliardini are doubtful due to head and ankle injuries respectively; however, Alieu Fadera (thigh), Mattia Felici (knee), Riyad Idrissi (knee) and M'Bala Nzola (thigh) are unavailable.

With two-goal forward Nzola absent, Daniel Maldini is the only available player to have scored more than once for the Sardinians this term and will aim to add to his five-goal tally against the visitors.

Antonio Raimondo was withdrawn after 30 minutes of Frosinone's eventual 2-0 victory over Torino, and the Capocannoniere contender is unavailable for the away side.

Raimondo's absence represents a significant blow, given that his eight goals account for a third of the Canaries' league tally of 24; Daniel Birligea should start, having recently scored twice in the 5-1 thrashing of Lecce on October 29.

Florian Grillitsch is also likely to miss out for the Canaries, having not played since September due to a thigh injury.

Caprile; Ze Pedro, Mina, Rodriguez, Sugawara; Adopo, Romano; Koffer, Maldini, Fazzini; Mendy

Palmisani; Oyono, Monterisi, Calvani, Bracaglia; Masini, Calo; Ghedjemis, Schmid, Kvernadze; Birligea

Raimondo's absence removes a significant source of goals for Frosinone, but Birligea's recent brace against Lecce suggests that the visitors retain enough firepower to trouble their struggling hosts.

Cagliari have repeatedly failed to protect promising positions during their four-match losing run, and the Canaries have our backing to end their long wait for victory in Sardinia.

#2383. Malagò: “Galliani? Non mi sentirete mai spendere parole non positive su di lui”

Le dichiarazioni del commissario straordinario tra la corsa alla Federcalcio e i retroscena sulle grandi scelte del passato

Giovanni Malagò, commissario e candidato alla presidenza FIGC, traccia il bilancio delle sfide del calcio italiano in alcune dichiarazioni riportate dall'ANSA, spaziando dalla sua candidatura e il rapporto con Galliani fino alle scelte sul commissariamento e le Olimpiadi.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Ho portato la candidatura perché mi sembrava doveroso dopo essere stato eletto il 22 giugno con una maggioranza importante e le tante vicende che sono successe tra pantouflage, tesseramento e dopo aver difeso l'autonomia del calcio. Galliani? Non posso che rispettarlo e stimarlo, quello che ci differenzia non è un tema anagrafico, non mi sentirete mai spendere parole non positive su di lui. Sicuramente è molto focalizzato sul mondo del calcio, io cerco di portare innovazioni e coraggio che vengono anche da altri mondi all'interno del calcio che penso di conoscere bene.

Se fossimo andati al voto in quella Giunta ne saremmo usciti tutti sconfitti. Quella è stata una soluzione che non so dirvi se la migliore, ma sicuramente la meno peggio. Non mi brucia, ma è come se avessi una ferita che si è cicatrizzata. Fu una scelta scellerata che portò a dei disastri a chi l'ha presa in termini politico-elettorali, però dopo sei mesi ci ritrovammo a occuparci di Milano Cortina. Latina fu fortemente penalizzata da quella decisione. Avremmo vinto e avremmo coinvolto questa zona come abbiamo fatto con tanti territori per Milano.

Il doppio successo ottenuto nella lunga finestra di settembre/ottobre con la Turchia e il pareggio in rimonta nel match di Saint-Denis con la Francia hanno permesso agli Azzurri di portarsi al secondo posto in classifica nel Gruppo 1 della Lega A a quota sette punti, tre in meno della capolista Francia e uno in più del Belgio: e proprio i Bleus e i Diavoli Rossi saranno gli avversari dell’Italia nelle sfide in programma giovedì 12 novembre allo stadio Giuseppe Meazza di Milano (emessi 57.000 biglietti, in vendita gli ultimi 3.000 tagliandi) e domenica 15 novembre allo stadio Re Baldovino di Bruxelles.

Quattro giovani sono alla prima chiamata in Nazionale maggiore: il centrocampista classe 2009 del Como Riccardo Cassano, il centrocampista classe 2008 del Parma Simone Lontani, l’attaccante classe 2006 del Milan Alphadjo Cisse e l’attaccante classe 2007 del Como Mattia Liberali.

Per quanto riguarda Cassano e Lontani, Mancini ha voluto convocarli per poterli osservare da vicino e per dar loro una preziosa opportunità per mettersi in mostra: non resteranno aggregati al gruppo per tutta la durata del raduno, ma nei prossimi giorni raggiungeranno le Nazionali Under 18 e Under 19, impegnate rispettivamente nel Mondiale al via il 19 novembre in Qatar e nella seconda fase delle qualificazioni al Campionato Europeo in programma dall’11 al 17 novembre nelle Marche.

#2378. Chelsea news: three positives and two disappointments... verdict on five players sent to Strasbourg

By Axel Clody | 05 Nov 2026 07:02

Very active in the last summer transfer window, Strasbourg signed five players from Chelsea. A few weeks after their arrival in Alsace, what is the verdict on their first steps with the club?

Revived by a home thrashing of Auxerre (5-1) after four consecutive defeats, Strasbourg have had a stop-start opening to the season.

Probably not helped by too big a squad overhaul in the last summer window, with close to £85m (€100m) spent and 17 signings registered, the Alsace club are suffering the full effect of a transfer mistake recently highlighted by Ismael Doukoure: a sizeable quota of foreign players and not a single signing with any Ligue 1 experience.

Among the summer arrivals, Hugo Oliveira’s side signed five players from Chelsea, the club with which Strasbourg share the same owner, BlueCo, in a collaboration designed to make transactions between the two sides easier.

They are Brazilian forward Deivid Washington, who cost £13.6m (€16m), English attacking midfielder Omari Kellyman, who cost £5.1m (€6m), Danish goalkeeper Filip Jorgensen on loan, Portuguese defensive midfielder Dario Essugo on loan and Swedish right-back Genesis Antwi on loan. Five players with radically different playing time and performances since the start of the 2026-27 season.

A few months after their arrival in France, what verdict can be drawn? Three positives and two disappointments stand out.

On the positive side, Dario Essugo has made four starts in seven appearances, with two goals and one assist. In terms of presence on the pitch, Filip Jorgensen outstrips the competition with nine starts in nine matches and a status strengthened by wearing the captain’s armband in three games. Although he has not yet kept a clean sheet, he has made 32 saves.

Genesis Antwi, for his part, has started eight times, with Hugo Oliveira only leaving him on the bench against Auxerre. The 19-year-old has just been rewarded for his good performances with a first call-up to Graham Potter’s Sweden squad for the upcoming international break and the Nations League matches against Bosnia-Herzegovina and Poland.

For the two other Chelsea recruits, however, the verdict is not very impressive. Deivid Washington, Strasbourg’s second most expensive signing of summer 2026, has so far been a flop: the Brazilian has still not played a single minute.

Finally, Omari Kellyman has only one appearance, for just six minutes, in a 3-0 defeat to Rennes on 25 October. In other words, the pile-up of players in Strasbourg’s squad has not stopped some from standing out while others remain on the sidelines. It is one way of weighing up the pros and cons of the club’s huge summer 2026 recruitment drive.

#2376. Barcelona: Raphinha to extend contract until 2030, three factors behind new deal

By Axel Clody | 05 Nov 2026 07:10

There could not be a better time for Raphinha to have his contract renewed by Barcelona. On Thursday, Barcelona will extend the deal of the Brazilian, who is the team’s current captain, until mid-2030, according to Trivela.

As well as being handed the captain’s armband this season, the Brazil international forward is arguably the best player in the world since the competitions began in August. Playing as a centre-forward, he has become a goal machine: 19 in only 13 matches in 2026-27.

It is a revolution the forward has experienced at the Catalan club since 2024. Signed in 2022, he was criticised and even questioned his future at the club, wavering over proposals from Saudi football.

The turning point came with the arrival of Hansi Flick, who convinced him to stay and turned him into one of the best players in the world, first on the left wing. In 2024-25, the German manager’s first year, he set career records for goals (34) and assists (22). After a poorer last year because of injuries, he has returned in full force this season.

His good form was one of the reasons for the renewal, as Trivela learned from an anonymous source who had contact with the negotiations. But it is not the only factor.

Raphinha and his staff understood that extending the contract was the best path given the moment he is experiencing and, in particular, because he is treated by the manager and the club as an important player. This is evident in every word Flick says about the Brazilian, always showering him with praise.

‘He is playing at a spectacular level. His strengths and characteristics fit our style perfectly. He always finds spaces to attack, and we also have players who can provide the final pass. He is very focused, he is our captain and he is showing his quality, which is important for us,’ Flick said after the 6-1 win over Alaves last weekend.

The captaincy mentioned by the manager was his own choice to reward the leadership of the number 11. Raphinha has always been a reference for younger players, which built trust in the dressing room, according to Trivela.

Having a foreigner as captain of one of the biggest clubs in the world is a weighty matter, but it came naturally. The Brazilian has won over the fans and created an identification that also made a difference in the renewal. For that reason there were no obstacles and the negotiation went smoothly for both sides.

When the new contract ends, Raphinha will be 33.

He is already the fourth Brazilian with the most goals for the club, with 94, level with Ronaldinho and behind only Neymar (105) and Rivaldo (130). In the overall ranking he is already in the top 25. In the Champions League, he has just equalled Robert Lewandowski, both with 23 goals, and is surpassed only by Luis Suarez and Lionel Messi.

He is a player who is increasingly becoming a Barcelona idol and, if he maintains his individual level and adds collective trophies, could finish the season as the best in the world.

#2371. Auxerre vs. Paris FC - prediction, team news, lineups

By Seye Omidiora | 05 Nov 2026 07:12, Last updated: 05 Nov 2026 07:14

Ten places separate Auxerre and Paris FC in the Ligue 1 table ahead of Saturday's matchday 10 fixture at Stade de l'Abbe-Deschamps.

Liam Rosenior's men could theoretically finish the weekend joint-top if they secure maximum points against the side with the division's worst defensive record and Paris Saint-Germain lose to Troyes.

Rosenior faced considerable criticism after his forgettable spell at Chelsea earlier this year, but the Englishman has Paris FC flying after nine matchdays.

Les Bleus sit fourth in the division with 17 points from a possible 27, level with third-placed Lyon, one behind second-placed Lille and just three adrift of local rivals PSG.

Much of their early-season success admittedly stems from their strong form at Stade Jean-Bouin, where they remain unbeaten after collecting 13 of a possible 15 points.

However, they have struggled to replicate those performances on their travels, losing half of their four away matches, including the last two against Lorient (1-0) and Lens (2-1).

Paris have not won away since early October's 3-2 success at Marseille, and a return of just four points from 12 on the road undermines their prospects of drawing level with PSG before Luis Enrique's men face Troyes later on Saturday.

Will Still's Auxerre have made a patchy start to the campaign, compounded by their failure to keep clean sheets in eight of their nine Ligue 1 fixtures.

The 14th-placed club's only shutout came in September's 1-0 victory over Nice, but Les Ajaistes have been unable to keep opponents at bay since.

That defensive weakness leaves Still's team vulnerable, and they enter Saturday's encounter having conceded 23 league goals, three more than bottom-placed Le Havre, further underlining their difficulties.

While their 13 goals exceed the totals of all but two bottom-half clubs, Brest (16) and Strasbourg (17), this weekend's hosts leave themselves with considerable work to do by conceding far too freely.

Nonetheless, eagle-eyed observers will note that Auxerre have conceded just five goals in front of their supporters while scoring as many themselves, offering encouragement ahead of matchday 10.

Given Paris' away struggles, Les Ajaistes, who have collected seven points from 12 on home soil, will back themselves to secure maximum points against Rosenior's high-flying side.

Lamfia Dioubate was dismissed after 11 minutes of the 5-1 defeat to Strasbourg last time out, leaving Auxerre without the midfielder.

While Sekou Fofana is touch-and-go due to a groin issue, Bryan Okoh, Remy Labeau Lascary and Assane Diousse are all sidelined for the hosts.

Cameron Archer leads Auxerre's scoring charts with six goals, three more than Danny Namaso, and the Englishman will aim to build on his prolific start to the campaign.

Paris, meanwhile, could be without Ligue 1's leading scorer Lassine Sinayoko, whose eight goals surpass everyone else's tally in the competition, while his five assists underline his dual threat.

Given that Sinayoko has scored half of Les Bleus' 16 league goals, the visitors may have to rely on alternative sources; only Maxime Lopez and Pablo Pagis have also scored more than once for Rosenior's side, leaving the manager short of proven match-winners.

Apart from Sinayoko, the capital club have several other injury doubts: Adama Camara (hamstring), Luca Koleosho (muscle), Jonathan Ikone (thigh) and Nhoa Sangui (muscle) could all miss out.

Nardi; Makosso, Tuanzebe, Akpa; Sy, Danois, Ahamada, Oppegard; Faivre, Namaso; Archer

Trapp; Traore, Chergui, Coppola, Otavio; Lopez, Lees Melou; Kebbal, Pagis, Simon; Sissoko

Auxerre's league-worst defensive record is concerning, but conceding just five goals across four home matches suggests that Saturday's assignment may be less straightforward than the table implies.

Paris have struggled to replicate their home performances on the road, and Sinayoko's potential absence could leave Rosenior's men settling for a point.

#2369. Lazio, Isaksen può guidare la squadra a Napoli: Gattuso pensa alla fascia

Il danese ha segnato quattro gol nelle ultime cinque partite e ha già colpito gli azzurri al Maradona nel dicembre 2024.

Gustav Isaksen potrebbe indossare la fascia di capitano della Lazio contro il Napoli. Zaccagni sarà assente al Maradona e i due vicecapitani, Cataldi e Marusic, sono attesi inizialmente in panchina. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, Gattuso sta pensando al danese, ma prima vuole confrontarsi con lo spogliatoio: nelle gerarchie del tecnico, la fascia dovrebbe andare al titolare con maggiore anzianità nel gruppo.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Ulteriori esami hanno ridimensionato lo stiramento al quadricipite di Zaccagni, aprendo uno spiraglio per il rientro subito dopo la sosta, forse contro il Lecce. Per la trasferta di Napoli, però, l’assenza del capitano impone a Gattuso una scelta diversa.

Arrivato dal Midtjylland nell’estate del 2023, Isaksen ha trovato continuità e ha imparato a incidere con maggiore frequenza. Ha conquistato anche i compagni: a Formello viene ormai considerato un punto di riferimento. La possibile promozione a capitano accompagnerebbe un percorso che lo ha portato a pesare sempre di più nella squadra.

In palestra è comparso un fotomontaggio con il corpo e la maglia di Arjen Robben ai tempi del Bayern e il volto di Isaksen. L’immagine richiama la sua prodezza contro il Cagliari, partita nella quale il danese è stato il migliore in campo. La Lazio ha chiuso la decima giornata da sola al primo posto e il suo contributo è stato ancora decisivo. A ricostruire il momento dell’esterno è Il Messaggero.

La crescita di Isaksen non riguarda soltanto le giocate sulla destra. Segna, accelera e supera l’avversario, ma partecipa anche alla fase difensiva e si sacrifica per la squadra. In campionato ha realizzato quattro gol, gli stessi di Noslin: è il miglior marcatore biancoceleste insieme al compagno. Gli mancano una rete per eguagliare le cinque della scorsa stagione.

È cambiata soprattutto la frequenza dei gol: prima segnava una volta ogni 460 minuti, ora una ogni 99. Sono quattro le reti nelle ultime cinque partite. Al bottino avrebbero potuto aggiungersi il colpo di testa finito sulla traversa contro il Parma, il tiro respinto dal palo contro il Monza e la conclusione salvata sulla linea da Kouadio.

Nel suo ottobre figurano anche un assist, il rigore conquistato a Reggio Emilia, otto falli subiti e tre cartellini provocati agli avversari. Tra questi c’è il secondo giallo rimediato da Obert contro il Cagliari, che ha costretto il giocatore a lasciare il campo in anticipo. Isaksen abbina alla velocità una maggiore capacità di scegliere quando partire e quale spazio attaccare.

Il danese è inoltre candidato al premio Player of the Month. Gli altri cinque nomi in corsa sono Calò del Frosinone, Douvikas del Como, Krstovic dell’Atalanta, McTominay del Napoli e Thuram dell’Inter.

#2368. Arsenal suffer fresh injury blow as Mikel Arteta reveals "some issues" after Slavia Prague win

By Lewis Blain | 05 Nov 2026 07:21

Arsenal have suffered a potential injury setback after Ezri Konsa was forced off during their 2-1 win over Slavia Prague in the Champions League on Wednesday evening.

Mikel Arteta confirmed the England defender was substituted because of a fitness issue, having appeared to struggle with his knee during the match.

However, the Gunners have received a timely boost with William Saliba now fit and available again after recovering from a long-term back injury.

Konsa started alongside Gabriel in the heart of Arsenal's defence but was withdrawn shortly after the hour mark following a difficult second half.

The centre-back was spotted feeling his knee during the opening period before continuing after the interval, although he was eventually replaced by Arteta.

The Arsenal manager confirmed the substitution was fitness-related rather than tactical, explaining that the defender had been playing a considerable amount of football recently.

"Obviously he's playing a lot," Arteta said after the match, via the Evening Standard. "He's played so many games in the last few weeks. He had some issues, and we decided to take him off."

Konsa's fitness will now be monitored, particularly with Arsenal continuing to juggle their domestic and European commitments.

There was better news regarding William Saliba, however, with the Frenchman returning to the matchday squad after missing the start of the season with a back injury.

Before the match, Arteta confirmed that the 25-year-old had completed 45 minutes in a behind-closed-doors friendly without any setbacks, and claimed that "he feels ready to go" and "feels really comfortable."

Saliba's return could hardly have come at a better time for the Gunners, particularly with Konsa now carrying a potential injury concern.

The summer signing has hugely impressed alongside Gabriel, but having another established centre-back available means Arteta is not suddenly short of options.

There would be some risk in throwing Saliba straight back into the starting XI against Hull City on Saturday, especially after such a lengthy absence.

However, the upcoming fixture could offer a relatively favourable opportunity to gradually reintegrate the Frenchman, provided Arteta is satisfied with his fitness.

Konsa's situation will need assessing before any decisions are made, but Saliba's availability gives Arsenal some welcome defensive insurance at an important stage of the campaign.

Sempre tra i giovani che si stanno segnalando in serie A c’è Robinson, fantasista del Monza ma di proprietà Southampton. Per il momento solo idee ma magari nei prossimi mesi l’Inter potrebbe stringere il cerchio e definire un obiettivo ben chiaro. E chiudiamo con la Roma. La priorità resta l’esterno. Endrick è il grande favorito. a ruota ci sono Kvernadze, Lewelling e Gittens, tutte ipotesi altrettanto concrete. In vista della prossima stagione però il club giallorosso guarda anche al centrocampo, visto che su Koné rimane alto il pressing di due grandi club come Chelsea e Arsenal. E così I riflettori si sono accesi su Issa Doumbia, che in estate lo Sporting Lisbona ha acquistato dal Venezia per una cifra di 20 milioni di euro più 6 di bonus. Il suo inserimento è stato molto positivo. Tra l’altra ha anche già segnato due reti in Champions League con Lens e la Shakhtar Donetsk. Adesso la sua valutazione è sicuramente cresciuta. Su di lui in Italia c’è anche la Juventus ma attenzione pure a Newcastle e Brentford. Lo Sporting lo ha blindato con un contratto fino al 2031 e clausola a 80 milioni. Un altro profilo monitorato è Gabriel Sara, brasiliano classe 1999 del Galatasaray. Ci pensa anche il Napoli ma vero pericolo arriva dalla Premier League dove Newcastle ed Everton sono pronti ad entrare in campo in maniera determinata.

#2366. Gabigol confessa: "Ecco perché ho scelto l'Inter e perché le cose non sono andate bene"

Gabigol confessa: "Ecco perché ho scelto l'Inter e perché le cose non sono andate bene"

Installa la nostra App gratuita e accedi più velocemente alle notizie

Mentre navighi nell'app, scorri il dito da sinistra a destra dello schermo per tornare alle pagine precedenti

Gabigol torna a parlare dell’Inter e lo fa con grande chiarezza. In una lunga intervista concessa a ge, l’attaccante brasiliano ha ripercorso diversi momenti della propria carriera, soffermandosi anche sul trasferimento a Milano e sull’esperienza vissuta in nerazzurro.

Scegli Fcinter1908.it come tuo sito preferito su Google: clicca qui

Il primo passaggio riguarda proprio la scelta di lasciare il Brasile per approdare in Europa.

�I miei genitori avevano comunque un peso, ma l’Inter fece un’offerta che era impossibile rifiutare. E io avevo anche voglia di giocare in Europa».

Gabigol, però, ammette che quella esperienza non riuscì a svilupparsi come sperato.

"Poi le cose non andarono bene. A quel punto andammo al Benfica perché pensavo che avrei avuto maggiori opportunità. Era una squadra del campionato portoghese, la lingua non sarebbe stata un problema e fu una decisione condivisa da tutti. Quando tornai al Santos nel 2018 mio padre ebbe un peso enorme nella decisione. Da quando arrivò il Flamengo, invece, cominciai a decidere completamente io. Quando si presentò il Flamengo dissi: “Devo giocare nel Flamengo”. Avevo sempre desiderato farlo. Quando arrivò quella possibilità non volli più ascoltare niente. Era il Flamengo. Credo che anche se fossero arrivati Real Madrid o Barcellona, sarei comunque andato al Flamengo"

Il brasiliano spiega che Santos e Flamengo hanno per lui un significato completamente diverso rispetto alle esperienze vissute in Europa.

�Se mi chiedeste di altre squadre, come l’Inter o il Benfica, il sentimento sarebbe completamente diverso. Anche con il Cruzeiro è diverso. Ma Santos e Flamengo sono le mie squadre».

Più avanti torna sul concetto, spiegando che il legame emotivo vissuto in Brasile non si è mai creato allo stesso modo durante il periodo europeo.

�Sono cose che semplicemente accadono, non puoi cercarle. Forse all’Inter e al Benfica questa connessione non si è creata allo stesso modo».

�Mio padre adorava il periodo in cui ero al Benfica. Io invece non amavo particolarmente il Portogallo. A Rio e qui a Santos, invece, per me è tutto perfetto».

�Ho avuto un fisioterapista personale da quando avevo otto anni. Mio padre si è sempre preso molta cura di questo aspetto».

E proprio a Milano il brasiliano era accompagnato da uno specialista di fiducia:

�Anche quando sono andato all’Inter, Thiago Lobo, che oggi lavora con Vinicius Junior, è venuto con me a Milano».

�Mi sento al massimo della mia carriera. Ho già tantissima esperienza alle spalle e ho vissuto tante situazioni nel calcio. Credo di aver affrontato praticamente tutto».

Il brasiliano ha poi ribadito la propria ambizione:

�Voglio essere il migliore, voglio migliorare, battere record e segnare in ogni partita».

Parole che raccontano un giocatore oggi molto diverso rispetto al ragazzo che lasciò il Santos per l’Inter. La scelta di Milano, come ha ammesso lui stesso, nacque da una proposta economica e sportiva impossibile da ignorare e dal desiderio di misurarsi con il calcio europeo.

#2361. Enzo Maresca now doubles down on his Gianluigi Donnarumma stance after latest Man City "mistake"

By Lewis Blain | 05 Nov 2026 07:47

Manchester City manager Enzo Maresca has defended Gianluigi Donnarumma once again following another costly mistake during their 3-2 win over RB Leipzig in the Champions League on Wednesday evening.

The Italian goalkeeper was criticised after allowing Ridle Baku's effort to beat him at the near post, adding to a growing list of errors this season.

However, Maresca has shown little indication that he intends to reconsider his first-choice goalkeeper ahead of Saturday's trip to face Nottingham Forest at the City Ground in the Premier League.

Donnarumma came under scrutiny after City's latest Champions League victory, having been unable to prevent Baku from equalising shortly after half-time.

The mistake followed previous errors against Aston Villa and Paris Saint-Germain, raising questions about the 27-year-old's consistency since arriving at the Etihad Stadium.

However, Maresca insisted that the incident was not a major concern, highlighting the goalkeeper's ability to produce important saves at the other end.

"It's not a concern, but it's a mistake," the City manager told reporters, via the BBC. "He recognises, he knows that it's a mistake. And now, in 48 hours, we have Nottingham Forest away, and we need to be focused on that."

Maresca was also asked whether the upcoming international break could provide an opportunity to reconsider his goalkeeping options, but again defended Donnarumma.

"I'm not thinking about the goalkeeper situation," he said. "We're talking about a mistake, but a few minutes before, he's done a save that any keeper in the world, they're not going to do that save!"

The City boss added that Donnarumma knows his performance was "not good" enough but believes he will improve in the coming weeks.

Donnarumma's recent errors are becoming difficult to ignore, particularly given his struggles with distribution and dealing with crosses into the penalty area.

However, Maresca's options behind the Italian are not particularly convincing, making a change difficult to justify.

City allowed James Trafford to leave during the summer, despite the Englishman's potential, and arguably made their own situation more complicated by not retaining a capable alternative.

Geronimo Rulli remains the back-up, and despite penalty heroics against Brighton & Hove Albion in the Carabao Cup, Maresca has already made clear that Donnarumma is his undisputed first choice.

The Italian's shot-stopping ability is also a major asset, as demonstrated by several outstanding interventions this season.

Despite the mistakes, it is difficult to envisage Maresca dropping Donnarumma anytime soon. The 6 foot 5 shot-stopper needs to improve his consistency, but City may have to persist with him given their current alternatives.

#2357. Nottingham Forest vs Manchester City - prediction, team news, lineups

By Oliver Thomas | 05 Nov 2026 08:00, Last updated: 06 Nov 2026 17:45

Second from bottom faces second from top in the Premier League on Saturday evening, as Nottingham Forest welcome Manchester City to the City Ground.

The two teams played out a 2-2 draw in their last meeting at the Etihad Stadium in March, with Elliot Anderson scoring a late equaliser for Forest before completing his big-money move to City in the summer.

Man City strengthened their hopes of securing automatic qualification for the Champions League last 16 with a 3-2 away victory over RB Leipzig on Wednesday, climbing into seventh place in the 36-team League Phase table at the halfway stage.

Manager Enzo Maresca has admitted that his team “struggled” in moments against Leipzig, but the German club’s nemesis Erling Haaland – wearing the captain’s armband – was on hand to slide home a 78th-minute match-winner for City and his 13th goal in just nine games against Die Roten Bullen.

The Citizens were made to work for their victory over Leipzig having previously suffered back-to-back Premier League defeats to Aston Villa and Brighton & Hove Albion, results that forced them to surrender top spot in the table to champions Arsenal.

City’s 24-game scoring streak in the Premier League was halted by a high-flying Seagulls side who battled to a narrow 1-0 victory at the Etihad last weekend, despite Maresca’s men registering 21 shots worth 1.92 expected goals (xG) and over 60% possession.

Not since February/March 2016 under Manuel Pellegrini have Man City lost three PL games in a row, and they head to the City Ground this weekend having won two of their previous four league visits (D1 L1) since Forest’s return to the top flight in 2022, most recently winning 2-1 in December last year.

Oliver Glasner has not made the desired impact as Nottingham Forest head coach, with his team winning only one of their opening nine Premier League games (D3 L5) and accumulating just a single point from their last 15 available, leaving them down in 19th place in the table.

Scoring goals has been the key issue for the Tricky Trees, who are the Premier League’s joint-lowest scorers (six) and have drawn a blank in five of their nine matches, despite registering the second-most shots per game in the division (16.9), only behind Manchester United (18.6).

A further 15 shots and just one on target in the 78th minute was recorded in last weekend’s 2-0 defeat at Brentford - another frustrating result that sparked a passionate post-match rant from Glasner, demanding his players to 'either buy in to changes or leave' to ensure that last season’s “s*** situation” does not repeat itself.

Forest’s hierarchy have reportedly welcomed Glasner’s strong words, but the real test is whether his misfiring squad will finally respond on the pitch. The Tricky Trees remains winless and goalless in five home fixtures this season, losing their last two to Coventry and Arsenal by a 1-0 scoreline.

Glasner now faces a daunting task against Man City, having lost four of his previous five league meetings with them (D1) during his time as Crystal Palace boss, conceding at least three goals in each defeat. However, Forest can draw confidence from their 1-0 home victory over City just two seasons ago, thanks to a late Callum Hudson-Odoi strike.

Nottingham Forest form (all competitions):

Nottingham Forest duo Nicolo Savona (knee) and Igor Jesus (calf) have both trained this week, but Saturday's game will come too soon for the former, while Nicolas Dominguez (muscle) is ruled out for around eight weeks.

Former Man City youngster Liam Delap has failed to score in his last five starts for Forest and is at risk of losing his centre-forward spot to Chris Wood, who is still waiting to start hist first league game of the season.

James McAtee – another ex-City player – has made a notable impression in a deeper midfield role where he is expected to continue, while Daniel Munoz – who has been managing a minor foot issue – and Ousmane Diomande will both be pushing for recalls in defence.

As for Man City, Maresca is not expected to make wholesale changes, but captain Ruben Dias and Josko Gvardiol are strong contenders to earn a recall in defence at the expense of Marc Guehi and Nico O'Reilly, who has been left out of the England squad due to fitness-related reasons.

Enzo Fernandez, who scored his first UCL goal for City in midweek, is the most likely midfielder to play alongside Anderson, though Ayyoub Bouaddi and Mateo Kovacic are two central options pushing to replace the Argentine.

Haaland – without a goal in his last four appearances against Forest – will lead the line once again and could be supported in attack by Iliman Ndiaye, Rayan Cherki and Jeremy Doku, the latter of whom began as a substitute against Leipzig.

Nottingham Forest possible starting lineup:

Sels; Cunha, Milenkovic, Murillo; Munoz, McAtee, Schlager, Williams; Ndoye, Gibbs-White; Delap

Manchester City possible starting lineup:

Donnarumma; Nunes, Khusanov, Dias, Gvardiol; Anderson, Fernandez; Ndiaye, Cherki, Doku; Haaland

Forest’s misfiring frontline is a growing concern for Glasner, but the hosts should take encouragement from Man City’s porous defence that has conceded 14 goals in their last seven games in all competitions.

A closely-contested battle could be in store this weekend, but we expect the Citizens to possess the clinical edge required to claim maximum points.

#2356. Casertana, Stellone e il mercato: “Qualche intervento mirato a gennaio”

Tre partite senza vittorie e una classifica che si è fatta meno comoda: la Casertana prepara la sfida al Casarano, valida per la 13ª giornata di Serie C, con il tecnico Roberto Stellone deciso a respingere ogni allarmismo. Nel frangente i falchetti hanno raccolto due pareggi e il ko sul campo del Giugliano, ma in conferenza stampa l’allenatore ha rivendicato fiducia nel gruppo e nelle prospettive stagionali.

Il primo messaggio è netto: “La classifica, sinceramente, non la guardo. Con la squadra che abbiamo e per come stiamo lavorando, il pensiero di doverci guardare alle spalle non mi sfiora minimamente. Non è presunzione, ma conoscenza del campionato e dei giocatori a disposizione”. Stellone rilegge le ultime uscite una per una: contro il Monopoli “una grande gara”, contro il Sorrento una buona reazione allo svantaggio, a Picerno una partita interpretata nel modo giusto. L’unica vera battuta d’arresto è quella di Giugliano: “Non parlerei di presunzione, ma di un calo di concentrazione”. E ancora: “Sull’impegno, invece, non ho nulla da rimproverare ai ragazzi”.

Contro il Casarano, che ha cambiato allenatore e sistema di gioco con Luciano Zauri in panchina, Stellone si aspetta una gara simile a quella con il Giugliano: “È una squadra che aspetta, concede il possesso e difende con un blocco basso. Dovremo essere intelligenti nel non concedere ripartenze e nel mantenere equilibrio mentre attacchiamo”.

Sul tema rotazioni il tecnico chiarisce che qualche punto fermo c’è: “Sugli esterni Vezzoni e Oukhadda rappresentano oggi le prime scelte, mentre davanti Manconi e Castelli sono due attaccanti sui quali faccio grande affidamento. Per il resto non sto facendo esperimenti”. L’obiettivo è un altro: “Più che trovare undici titolari fissi, sto cercando di individuare il giocatore che sappia fare da raccordo tra centrocampo e attacco”.

Capitolo De Lucia, finito nel mirino dopo il gol subito a Giugliano. Stellone lo difende: “Victor è un portiere forte, che conosco dai tempi di Frosinone, e non lo cambierei con nessun altro della Serie C. È un ragazzo serio, attaccato alla maglia e molto coinvolto nel progetto. Ho piena fiducia in lui”. A chi chiama in causa i cambi in difesa risponde con i dati: “Da quando sono arrivato siamo tra le squadre che concedono meno occasioni e meno tiri in porta agli avversari”.

Lo sguardo è già rivolto al futuro: “La Casertana, alla lunga, sarà davanti. Ci mancano ancora alcuni passaggi per diventare la squadra che vogliamo essere, ma sono molto fiducioso. Con il lavoro e con qualche intervento mirato a gennaio nei ruoli in cui siamo numericamente più scoperti, possiamo crescere ancora tanto”.

In infermeria, l’unico da valutare è Belli, che ha accusato un piccolo affaticamento muscolare: “Decideremo con lo staff medico se sarà opportuno aggregarlo al gruppo o concedergli un turno di riposo. Per il resto stanno tutti bene”.

#2354. Napoli in ritiro verso la Lazio: Allegri mantiene la fiducia del club

La decisione di De Laurentiis arriva dopo il 4-1 subito dal Porto e la settima sconfitta in quattordici gare.

Il Napoli andrà in ritiro da questa sera per preparare la partita di domenica contro la Lazio di Rino Gattuso. Secondo quanto riportato da Il Messaggero, Aurelio De Laurentiis ha deciso di intervenire dopo il 4-1 subito sul campo del Porto. Per il momento non sono previsti cambi in panchina: Max Allegri conserva la fiducia della società.

CLICCA QUI E SEGUI LAZIOPRESS.IT ANCHE SU INSTAGRAM

Alla sconfitta in Portogallo è seguito un giorno di silenzio, dopo le dichiarazioni rilasciate allo stadio Dragao. Il Napoli ha perso sette delle prime quattordici gare stagionali. Contro il Porto si è presentato con un gruppo ridotto dai numerosi infortuni e con i nervi tesi per i risultati.

Le parole di Politano hanno mostrato le difficoltà dello spogliatoio: «Vorrei che la faccia non ce la mettessimo soltanto noi italiani». La squadra fatica a ritrovare compattezza e Allegri, a sua volta, è finito al centro delle critiche.

Secondo Il Messaggero, il Napoli non ha dato una sensazione di solidità e spesso è calato dopo un tempo di gioco. È accaduto anche al di là dell’emergenza infortuni, destinata a proseguire nella sfida con i biancocelesti.

De Laurentiis è infuriato. In estate aveva sostenuto la forza dell’organico e la necessità di intervenire poco sul mercato, confidando nella qualità della rosa. A distanza di pochi mesi, diversi titolari attraversano invece un periodo difficile.

Rrahmani appare lontano dal rendimento autorevole delle ultime due stagioni. Di Lorenzo, ora assente, Politano e Anguissa hanno avuto prestazioni altalenanti, così come Kevin De Bruyne. Il belga suscita perplessità anche sotto il profilo caratteriale: si espone poco nel ruolo di leader e resta uno degli interrogativi della squadra.

La società non pensa, al momento, a un ribaltone tecnico, ma ha scelto il ritiro per tutto il gruppo come segnale in vista della Lazio. Il Napoli è rientrato dal Portogallo soltanto ieri pomeriggio; i confronti interni sono stati rinviati a oggi. Ad Allegri spetterà riportare tranquillità in un ambiente deluso. Mercoledì sera i tifosi hanno contestato la squadra al Dragao e al Maradona si attende una reazione.

#2353. Nottingham Forest lineup vs. Man City: Predicted XI for Premier League clash

By Oliver Thomas | 05 Nov 2026 08:05, Last updated: 06 Nov 2026 15:43

Oliver Glasner has promised "changes" at Nottingham Forest ahead of Saturday’s Premier League home clash with Manchester City, but it remains to be seen just how radically he intends to alter his starting lineup.

The Tricky Trees are languishing in the relegation zone after losing 2-0 at Brentford last weekend and failing to score for the fifth time in nine top-flight matches this season.

Summer signing Liam Delap is without a goal in his last five starts and the former Man City youngster is in danger of losing his place up front to Chris Wood, who is still waiting for his first start of the season.

Morgan Gibbs-White and Dan Ndoye are likely to retain their starting spots in attack, though Callum Hudson-Odoi – who scored in a 1-0 home win for Forest against City in March 2025 – will be pushing to replace the latter.

James McAtee – another ex-Citizens player – has impressed in centre-midfield this term and is set to continue in the first XI alongside Xaver Schlager, while Daniel Munoz should be fit to replace Ola Aina at right wing-back. Neco Williams, meanwhile, will likely start again on the left flank.

While Ousmane Diomande is a contender to replace either Nikola Milenkovic or Jair Cunha at centre-back, Murillo is expected to continue on the left side of a back three, with goalkeeper Matz Sels standing between the sticks.

Elsewhere, Nicolas Dominguez is ruled out for around eight weeks with a muscle problem, joining Nicolo Savona (knee) in the treatment room, while forward Igor Jesus (calf) will be assessed ahead of kickoff.

Nottingham Forest possible starting lineup:

Sels; Cunha, Milenkovic, Murillo; Munoz, McAtee, Schlager, Williams; Ndoye, Gibbs-White; Delap
